Grounding(\n                    GroundingKind.SOURCE,\n                    path,\n                    f\"active as of {EVAL_DATE}\",\n                )\n            ],\n        )\n\n\ndef load_candidates(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    registry_norms: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for row in tv.query('SELECT company_id, legal_name FROM registry_record'):\n        registry_norms[row[\"company_id\"]] = normalize_name(row[\"legal_name\"])\n\n    contract_counterparty_norm: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for row in tv.query(\n        \"SELECT contract_id,   Grounding(\n                            GroundingKind.SOURCE,\n                            f\"sources/contracts/{CONTRACT_SPECS[cluster['contract']]['file']}\",\n                            \"counterparty\",\n                        )\n                    ],\n                )\n\n\ndef register_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT contract_id AS contract, clause_kind\n        FROM contract_clause\n        WHERE clause_kind IN (\n            'change_of_control_consent',\n            'change_of_control_termination',\n            'assignment_consent_required',\n            'assignment_notice_required',\n            'assignment_competitor_prohibition'\n        )\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"contract_clause\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"invoice_contract_link\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        WITH invoice_billing AS (\n            SELECT invoice_id AS invoice,\n                   'billing:' || billed_name AS billing_ref\n            FROM invoice_record\n        ),\n        asserted_counterparty_contract AS (\n            SELECT ij.left AS counterparty, ctc.contract AS contract\n            FROM identity_judgment ij\n            JOIN counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n              ON ctc.counterparty_id = ij.left_id\n             AND ctc.contract_id = ij.right_id\n            WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n            UNION\n            SELECT ij.right AS counterparty, ctc.contract AS contract\n            FROM identity_judgment ij\n            JOIN counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n              ON ctc.counterparty_id = ij.right_id\n             AND ctc.contract_id = ij.left_id\n            WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n            UNION\n            SELECT ctc.counterparty_id AS counterparty, ctc.contract_id AS contract\n            FROM counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n            WHERE NOT EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                WHERE (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                   OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n            )\n        ),\n        links AS (\n            SELECT ib.invoice,\n                   acc.contract,\n                   CASE\n                     WHEN EXISTS (\n                       SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                       WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n                         AND (\n                           (ij.left_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.right_id = acc.contract)\n                           OR (ij.right_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.left_id = acc.contract)\n                         )\n                     ) THEN 'asserted'\n                     WHEN EXISTS (\n                       SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                       WHERE ij.disposition = 'UNRESOLVED'\n                         AND (\n                           (ij.left_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.right_id = acc.contract)\n                           OR (ij.right_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.left_id = acc.contract)\n                         )\n                     ) THEN 'unresolved'\n                     WHEN ib.billing_ref = acc.counterparty THEN 'asserted'\n                     ELSE 'unresolved'\n                   END AS association\n            FROM invoice_billing ib\n            JOIN asserted_counterparty_contract acc\n              ON acc.counterparty = ib.billing_ref\n        )\n        SELECT invoice, contract, association FROM links\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"identity_judgment\",\n            \"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_a_invoice\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT ir.invoice_id AS invoice,\n               ir.billed_name,\n               ir.amount,\n               ir.currency,\n               ir.period,\n               ir.status,\n               icl.contract_id AS contract,\n               icl.association\n        FROM invoice_record ir\n        JOIN invoice_contract_link icl ON icl.invoice_id = ir.invoice_id\n        JOIN contract_active ca ON ca.contract_id = icl.contract_id AND ca.active = 1\n        WHERE EXISTS (\n            SELECT 1 FROM acquisition_relevant_clause arc\n            WHERE arc.contract_id = icl.contract_id\n        )\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"invoice_contract_link\",\n            \"contract_active\",\n            \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_b_link\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT CASE\n                 WHEN ij.left_id LIKE 'crm:%' OR ij.left_id LIKE 'billing:%'\n                   OR ij.left_id LIKE 'registry:%' OR ij.left_id LIKE 'contract:%'\n                 THEN ij.left_id\n                 ELSE ij.left\n               END AS left,\n               CASE\n                 WHEN ij.right_id LIKE 'crm:%' OR ij.right_id LIKE 'billing:%'\n                   OR ij.right_id LIKE 'registry:%' OR ij.right_id LIKE 'contract:%'\n                 THEN ij.right_id\n                 ELSE ij.right\n               END AS right,\n               ij.disposition AS epistemic\n        FROM identity_judgment ij\n        WHERE ij.left_id LIKE 'crm:%' OR ij.left_id LIKE 'billing:%'\n           OR ij.left_id LIKE 'registry:%' OR ij.left_id LIKE 'contract:%'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"identity_judgment\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_c_dependency\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        WITH open_invoices AS (\n            SELECT 'billing:' || billed_name AS counterparty,\n                   invoice_id AS invoice,\n                   substr(invoice_id, 9) AS invoice_short\n            FROM invoice_record\n            WHERE status = 'open'\n        ),\n        obligations AS (\n            SELECT contract_id AS contract, clause_kind AS kind\n            FROM contract_clause\n            WHERE clause_kind = 'exclusivity'\n            UNION\n            SELECT contract_id AS contract, term_kind AS kind\n            FROM contract_term_kind\n            WHERE term_kind IN ('auto_renewal', 'rolling_term')\n        ),\n        active_contracts AS (\n            SELECT contract_id AS contract FROM contract_active WHERE active = 1\n        ),\n        contract_counterparty AS (\n            SELECT ctc.contract_id AS contract,\n                   ctc.counterparty_id AS counterparty\n            FROM counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n            WHERE EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n                  AND (\n                    (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                    OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                  )\n            )\n            OR NOT EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                WHERE (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                   OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n            )\n        ),\n        base AS (\n            SELECT cc.counterparty,\n                   cc.contract,\n                   GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT oi.invoice_short) AS open_invoice_ids,\n                   GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT ob.kind) AS obligation_kinds\n            FROM contract_counterparty cc\n            JOIN active_contracts ac ON ac.contract = cc.contract\n            JOIN obligations ob ON ob.contract = cc.contract\n            LEFT JOIN open_invoices oi ON oi.counterparty = cc.counterparty\n            GROUP BY cc.counterparty, cc.contract\n            HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT ob.kind) >= 1\n               AND (COUNT(DISTINCT oi.invoice_short) > 0\n                    OR EXISTS (\n                        SELECT 1 FROM contract_term_kind ctk\n                        WHERE ctk.contract_id = cc.contract\n                          AND ctk.term_kind IN ('auto_renewal', 'rolling_term')\n                    ))\n        )\n        SELECT counterparty,\n               contract,\n               COALESCE(open_invoice_ids, '') AS open_invoice_ids,\n               obligation_kinds,\n               CASE\n                 WHEN EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                   WHERE ij.disposition = 'UNRESOLVED'\n                     AND (ij.left_id = counterparty OR ij.right_id = counterparty)\n                 ) THEN 'unresolved'\n                 ELSE 'dependent'\n               END AS            \"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\",\n            \"identity_judgment\",\n        ],\n    )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n )\n\n\ndef load_contracts(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    for contract_id, spec in CONTRACT_SPECS.items():\n        ref = f\"contract:{contract_id}\"\n        path = \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT contract_id AS contract, clause_kind\n        FROM contract_clause\n        WHERE clause_kind IN (\n            'change_of_control_consent',\n            'change_of_control_termination',\n            'assignment_consent_required',\n            'assignment_notice_required',\n            'assignment_competitor_prohibition'\n        )\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"contract_clause\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"invoice_contract_link\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n asserted_counterparty_contract AS (\n            SELECT ij.left AS counterparty, ctc.contract AS contract\n            FROM identity_judgment ij\n            JOIN counterparty_to_contract_candidate AS counterparty, ctc.contract AS contract\n            FROM identity_judgment ij\n            JOIN counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n              ON ctc.counterparty_id = ij.right_id\n             AND ctc.contract_id = ij.left_id\n            WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n            UNION\n            SELECT ctc.counterparty_id AS counterparty, ctc.contract_id AS contract\n            FROM counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n            WHERE NOT EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                WHERE (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                   OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n            )\n        ),\n        links AS (\n            SELECT ib.invoice,\n                   acc.contract,\n                   CASE\n                     WHEN EXISTS (\n                       SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                       WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n                         AND (\n                           (ij.left_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.right_id = acc.contract)\n                           OR (ij.right_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.left_id = acc.contract)\n                         )\n                     ) THEN 'asserted'\n                     WHEN EXISTS (\n                       SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                       WHERE ij.disposition = 'UNRESOLVED'\n                         AND (\n                           (ij.left_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.right_id = acc.contract)\n                           OR (ij.right_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.left_id = acc.contract)\n                         )\n                     ) THEN 'unresolved'\n                     WHEN ib.billing_ref = acc.counterparty THEN 'asserted'\n                     ELSE 'unresolved'\n                   END AS association\n            FROM invoice_billing ib\n            JOIN asserted_counterparty_contract acc\n              ON acc.counterparty = ib.billing_ref\n        )\n        SELECT invoice, contract, association FROM links\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"identity_judgment\",\n            \"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_a_invoice\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT ir.invoice_id AS invoice,\n               ir.billed_name,\n               ir.amount,\n               ir.currency,\n               ir.period,\n               ir.status,\n               icl.contract_id AS contract,\n               icl.association\n        FROM invoice_record ir\n        JOIN invoice_contract_link icl ON icl.invoice_id = ir.invoice_id\n        JOIN \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_b_link\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT CASE\n                 WHEN ij.left_id LIKE 'crm:%' OR invoice_id AS invoice,\n                   substr(invoice_id, 9) AS invoice_short\n            FROM invoice_record\n            WHERE status = 'open'\n        ),\n        obligations AS (\n            SELECT contract_id AS contract, clause_kind AS kind\n            FROM contract_clause\n            WHERE clause_kind = 'exclusivity'\n            UNION\n            SELECT contract_id AS contract, term_kind AS kind\n            FROM contract_term_kind\n            WHERE term_kind IN ('auto_renewal', 'rolling_term')\n        ),\n        active_contracts AS (\n            SELECT contract_id AS contract FROM contract_active WHERE active = 1\n        ),\n        contract_counterparty AS (\n            SELECT ctc.contract_id AS contract,\n                   ctc.counterparty_id AS counterparty\n            FROM counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n            WHERE EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n                  AND (\n                    (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                    OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                  )\n            )\n            OR NOT EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                WHERE (ij.left_id =      SELECT cc.counterparty,\n                   cc.contract,\n                   GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT oi.invoice_short) AS open_invoice_ids,\n                   GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT ob.kind) AS obligation_kinds\n            FROM contract_counterparty cc\n            JOIN active_contracts ac ON ac.contract = cc.contract\n            JOIN obligations ob ON ob.contract = cc.contract\n            LEFT JOIN open_invoices oi ON oi.counterparty = cc.counterparty\n            GROUP BY cc.counterparty, cc.contract\n            HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT ob.kind) >= 1\n               AND (COUNT(DISTINCT oi.invoice_short) > 0\n                    OR EXISTS (\n                        SELECT 1 FROM contract_term_kind ctk\n                        WHERE ctk.contract_id = cc.contract\n                          AND ctk.term_kind IN ('auto_renewal', 'rolling_term')\n                    ))\n        )\n        SELECT counterparty,\n               contract,\n               COALESCE(open_invoice_ids, '') AS open_invoice_ids,\n               obligation_kinds,\n               CASE\n                 WHEN EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                   WHERE ij.disposition = 'UNRESOLVED'\n                     AND (ij.left_id = counterparty OR ij.right_id = counterparty)\n                 ) THEN 'unresolved'\n                 ELSE 'dependent'\n               END AS            \"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\",\n            \"identity_judgment\",\n        ],\n    )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n AND ij.left_id = acc.contract)\n+                         )\n+                     ) THEN 'unresolved'\n+                     WHEN ib.billing_ref = acc.counterparty THEN 'asserted'\n+                     ELSE 'unresolved'\n+                   END AS association\n+            FROM invoice_billing ib\n+            JOIN asserted_counterparty_contract acc\n+              ON acc.counterparty = \"purpose_c_dependency\",\n+        sql=\"\"\"\n+        WITH open_invoices AS (\n+            SELECT 'billing:' || billed_name AS counterparty,\n+                   invoice_id AS invoice,\n+ load_candidates(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    registry_norms: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for row in tv.query('SELECT company_id, legal_name FROM registry_record'):\n        registry_norms[row[\"company_id\"]] = normalize_name(row[\"legal_name\"])\n\n    contract_counterparty_norm: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for row in tv.query(\n        \"SELECT contract_id, counterparty_text FROM contract_record\"\n    ):\n        contract_counterparty_norm[row[\"contract_id\"]] = normalize_name(\n            row[\"counterparty_text\"]\n        )\n\n    for cluster in COMMERCIAL_CLUSTERS:\n        crm_ref = f\"crm:{cluster['crm']}\"\n        billing_ref = f\"billing:{cluster['billing_name']}\"\n        contract_ref = f\"contract:{cluster['contract']}\"\n        tv.assert_tuple(\n            \"crm_to_billing_candidate\",\n            {\"crm_account\": crm_ref, \"billing_name\": billing_ref},\n            grounding=[\n                Grounding(\n                    GroundingKind.SOURCE,\n                    \"commercial_notes.md\",\n                    f\"cluster {cluster['crm']}\",\n                )\n            ],\n        )\n        billing_norm = normalize_name(cluster[\"billing_name\"])\n        for company_number in cluster[\"registry\"]:\n            registry_ref = f\"registry:{company_number}\"\n            reg_norm = registry_norms[registry_ref]\n            if billing_norm == reg_norm or billing_norm in reg_norm or reg_norm in billing_norm:\n                tv.assert_tuple(\n                    \"billing_to_registry_candidate\",\n                    {\n                        \"billing_name\": billing_ref,\n                        \"registry_company\": registry_ref,\n                    },\n                    grounding=[\n                        Grounding(\n                            GroundingKind.SOURCE,\n                            \"company_registry.csv\",\n in (\n            (billing_ref, billing_norm),\n            (crm_ref, crm_norm),\n        ):\n            if norm == cp_norm or norm in cp_norm or cp_norm in norm:\n                tv.assert_tuple(\n                    \"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\",\n                    {\"counterparty\": counterparty_ref, \"contract\": contract_ref},\n                    grounding=[\n                        Grounding(\n                            GroundingKind.SOURCE,\n                            f\"sources/contracts/{CONTRACT_SPECS[cluster['contract']]['file']}\",\n                            \"counterparty\",\n                        )\n                    ],\n                )\n\n\ndef register_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT contract_id AS contract, clause_kind\n        FROM contract_clause\n        WHERE clause_kind IN (\n            'change_of_control_consent',\n            'change_of_control_termination',\n            'assignment_consent_required',\n            'assignment_notice_required',\n            'assignment_competitor_prohibition'\n        )\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"contract_clause\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"invoice_contract_link\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        WITH invoice_billing AS (\n            SELECT invoice_id AS invoice,\n                   'billing:' || billed_name AS billing_ref\n            FROM invoice_record\n        ),\n        asserted_counterparty_contract AS (\n            SELECT ij.left AS counterparty, ctc.contract AS contract\n            FROM identity_judgment ij\n            JOIN counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n              ON ctc.counterparty_id = ij.left_id\n             AND ctc.contract_id = ij.right_id\n            WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n            UNION\n            SELECT ij.right AS counterparty, ctc.contract AS contract\n            FROM identity_judgment ij\n            JOIN counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n              ON ctc.counterparty_id = ij.right_id\n             AND ctc.contract_id = ij.left_id\n            WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n            UNION\n            SELECT ctc.counterparty_id AS counterparty, ctc.contract_id AS contract\n            FROM counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n            WHERE NOT EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                WHERE (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                   OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n            )\n        ),\n        links AS (\n            SELECT ib.invoice,\n                   acc.contract,\n                   CASE\n                     WHEN EXISTS (\n                       SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                       WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n                         AND (\n                           (ij.left_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.right_id = acc.contract)\n                           OR (ij.right_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.left_id = acc.contract)\n                         )\n                     ) THEN 'asserted'\n                     WHEN EXISTS (\n                       SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                       WHERE ij.disposition = 'UNRESOLVED'\n                         AND (\n                           (ij.left_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.right_id = acc.contract)\n                           OR (ij.right_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.left_id = acc.contract)\n                         )\n                     ) THEN 'unresolved'\n                     WHEN ib.billing_ref = acc.counterparty THEN 'asserted'\n                     ELSE 'unresolved'\n                   END AS association\n            FROM invoice_billing ib\n            JOIN asserted_counterparty_contract acc\n              ON acc.counterparty = ib.billing_ref\n        )\n        SELECT invoice, contract, association FROM links\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"identity_judgment\",\n            \"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_a_invoice\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT ir.invoice_id AS invoice,\n               ir.billed_name,\n               ir.amount,\n               ir.currency,\n               ir.period,\n               ir.status,\n               icl.contract_id AS contract,\n               icl.association\n        FROM invoice_record ir\n        JOIN invoice_contract_link icl ON icl.invoice_id = ir.invoice_id\n        JOIN contract_active ca ON ca.contract_id = icl.contract_id AND ca.active = 1\n        WHERE EXISTS (\n            SELECT 1 FROM acquisition_relevant_clause arc\n            WHERE arc.contract_id = icl.contract_id\n        )\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"invoice_contract_link\",\n            \"contract_active\",\n            \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_b_link\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT CASE\n                 WHEN ij.left_id LIKE 'crm:%' OR ij.left_id LIKE 'billing:%'\n                   OR ij.left_id LIKE 'registry:%' OR ij.left_id LIKE 'contract:%'\n                 THEN ij.left_id\n                 ELSE ij.left\n               END AS left,\n               CASE\n                 WHEN ij.right_id LIKE 'crm:%' OR ij.right_id LIKE 'billing:%'\n                   OR ij.right_id LIKE 'registry:%' OR ij.right_id LIKE 'contract:%'\n                 THEN ij.right_id\n                 ELSE ij.right\n               END AS right,\n               ij.disposition AS epistemic\n        FROM identity_judgment ij\n        WHERE ij.left_id LIKE 'crm:%' OR ij.left_id LIKE 'billing:%'\n           OR ij.left_id LIKE 'registry:%' OR ij.left_id LIKE 'contract:%'\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"identity_judgment\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_c_dependency\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        WITH open_invoices AS (\n            SELECT 'billing:' || billed_name AS counterparty,\n                   invoice_id AS invoice,\n                   substr(invoice_id, 9) AS invoice_short\n            FROM invoice_record\n            WHERE status = 'open'\n        ),\n        obligations AS (\n            SELECT contract_id AS contract, clause_kind AS kind\n            FROM contract_clause\n            WHERE clause_kind = 'exclusivity'\n            UNION\n            SELECT contract_id AS contract, term_kind AS kind\n            FROM contract_term_kind\n            WHERE term_kind IN ('auto_renewal', 'rolling_term')\n        ),\n        active_contracts AS (\n            SELECT contract_id AS contract FROM contract_active WHERE active = 1\n        ),\n        contract_counterparty AS (\n            SELECT ctc.contract_id AS contract,\n                   ctc.counterparty_id AS counterparty\n            FROM counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n            WHERE EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n                  AND (\n                    (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                    OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                  )\n            )\n            OR NOT EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                WHERE (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                   OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n            )\n        ),\n        base AS (\n            SELECT cc.counterparty,\n                   cc.contract,\n                   GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT oi.invoice_short) AS open_invoice_ids,\n                   GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT ob.kind) AS obligation_kinds\n            FROM contract_counterparty cc\n            JOIN active_contracts ac ON ac.contract = cc.contract\n            JOIN obligations ob ON ob.contract = cc.contract\n            LEFT JOIN open_invoices oi ON oi.counterparty = cc.counterparty\n            GROUP BY cc.counterparty, cc.contract\n            HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT ob.kind) >= 1\n               AND (COUNT(DISTINCT oi.invoice_short) > 0\n                    OR EXISTS (\n                        SELECT 1 FROM contract_term_kind ctk\n                        WHERE ctk.contract_id = cc.contract\n                          AND ctk.term_kind IN ('auto_renewal', 'rolling_term')\n                    ))\n        )\n        SELECT counterparty,\n               contract,\n               COALESCE(open_invoice_ids, '') AS open_invoice_ids,\n               obligation_kinds,\n               CASE\n                 WHEN EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                   WHERE ij.disposition = 'UNRESOLVED'\n                     AND (ij.left_id = counterparty OR ij.right_id = counterparty)\n                 ) THEN 'unresolved'\n                 ELSE 'dependent'\n               END AS status\n        FROM base\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"contract_clause\",\n            \"contract_term_kind\",\n            \"contract_active\",\n            \"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\",\n            \"identity_judgment\",\n        ],\n    )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE, universe=\"contract_clause\"\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n \"purpose_b_link\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE, universe=\"identity_judgment\"\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"purpose_c_dependency\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE, universe=\"contract_active\"\n        ),\n    )\n\n\ndef compile_mechanical(tv: TaskView) -> \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT contract_id, clause_kind\n        FROM contract_clause\n        WHERE clause_kind IN (\n            'change_of_control_consent',\n \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"contract_clause\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"invoice_contract_link\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        WITH invoice_billing AS (\n            SELECT                  SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                   WHERE ij.disposition = 'UNRESOLVED'\n                     AND (\n                       (ij.left_id = ib.billing_ref AND (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                    OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                  )\n              )\n        ),\n        base AS (\n            SELECT bc.counterparty,\n                   bc.contract_id,\n                   GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT oi.invoice_short) AS open_invoice_ids,\n JOIN obligations ob ON ob.contract_id = bc.contract_id\n            LEFT JOIN open_invoices oi ON oi.counterparty = bc.counterparty\n            GROUP BY bc.counterparty, bc.contract_id\n SELECT counterparty,\n               contract_id,\n               COALESCE(open_invoice_ids, '') AS open_invoice_ids,\n               obligation_kinds,\n               CASE\n                 WHEN counterparty)\n                 ) THEN 'unresolved'\n                 ELSE 'dependent'\n               END AS status\n        FROM base\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"contract_clause\",\n            \"contract_term_kind\",\n            \"contract_active\",\n            \"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\",\n            \"identity_judgment\",\n        ],\n    )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT contract_id, clause_kind\n        FROM contract_clause\n        WHERE clause_kind IN (\n            'change_of_control_consent',\n            'change_of_control_termination',\n            'assignment_consent_required',\n            'assignment_notice_required',\n            'assignment_competitor_prohibition'\n        )\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"contract_clause\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"invoice_contract_link\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        WITH invoice_billing AS (\n            SELECT invoice_id, 'billing:' || billed_name AS billing_ref\n            FROM invoice_record\n        ),\n        billing_contract AS (\n            SELECT ctc.counterparty_id AS billing_ref, ctc.contract_id\n            FROM counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n            WHERE ctc.counterparty_id LIKE 'billing:%'\n              AND NOT EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                WHERE ij.disposition = 'DISTINCT'\n                  AND (\n                    (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                    OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                  )\n              )\n              AND (\n                EXISTS (\n                  SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                  WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n                    AND (\n                      (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                      OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                    )\n                )\n                OR NOT EXISTS (\n                  SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                  WHERE (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                     OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                )\n              )\n        )\n        SELECT ib.invoice_id,\n               bc.contract_id,\n               CASE\n                 WHEN EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                   WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n                     AND (\n                       (ij.left_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.right_id = bc.contract_id)\n                       OR (ij.right_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.left_id = bc.contract_id)\n                     )\n                 ) THEN 'asserted'\n                 WHEN EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                   WHERE ij.disposition = 'UNRESOLVED'\n                     AND (\n                       (ij.left_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.right_id = bc.contract_id)\n                       OR (ij.right_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.left_id = bc.contract_id)\n                     )\n                 ) THEN 'unresolved'\n                 ELSE 'asserted'\n               END AS association\n        FROM invoice_billing ib\n        JOIN billing_contract bc ON bc.billing_ref = ib.billing_ref\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"identity_judgment\",\n            \"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_a_invoice\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT ir.invoice_id,\n               ir.billed_name,\n               ir.amount,\n               ir.currency,\n               ir.period,\n               ir.status,\n               icl.contract_id,\n               icl.association\n        FROM invoice_record ir\n        JOIN invoice_contract_link icl ON icl.invoice_id = ir.invoice_id\n        JOIN contract_active ca ON ca.contract_id = icl.contract_id AND ca.active = 1\n        WHERE EXISTS (\n            SELECT 1 FROM acquisition_relevant_clause arc\n            WHERE arc.contract_id = icl.contract_id\n        )\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"invoice_contract_link\",\n            \"contract_active\",\n            \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_b_link\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT ij.left_id AS left,\n               ij.right_id AS right,\n               ij.disposition AS epistemic\n        FROM identity_judgment ij\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"identity_judgment\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_c_dependency\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        WITH open_invoices AS (\n            SELECT 'billing:' || billed_name AS counterparty,\n                   replace(invoice_id, 'invoice:', '') AS invoice_short\n            FROM invoice_record\n            WHERE status = 'open'\n        ),\n        obligations AS (\n            SELECT contract_id, clause_kind AS kind\n            FROM contract_clause\n            WHERE clause_kind = 'exclusivity'\n            UNION\n            SELECT contract_id, term_kind AS kind\n            FROM contract_term_kind\n            WHERE term_kind IN ('auto_renewal', 'rolling_term')\n        ),\n        billing_contract AS (\n            SELECT ctc.counterparty_id AS counterparty, ctc.contract_id\n            FROM counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n            WHERE ctc.counterparty_id LIKE 'billing:%'\n              AND EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n                  AND (\n                    (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                    OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                  )\n              )\n        ),\n        base AS (\n            SELECT bc.counterparty,\n                   bc.contract_id,\n                   GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT oi.invoice_short) AS open_invoice_ids,\n                   GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT ob.kind) AS obligation_kinds\n            FROM billing_contract bc\n            JOIN contract_active ca ON ca.contract_id = bc.contract_id AND ca.active = 1\n            JOIN obligations ob ON ob.contract_id = bc.contract_id\n            LEFT JOIN open_invoices oi ON oi.counterparty = bc.counterparty\n            GROUP BY bc.counterparty, bc.contract_id\n            HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT ob.kind) >= 1\n               AND (\n                 COUNT(DISTINCT oi.invoice_short) > 0\n                 OR EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM contract_term_kind ctk\n                   WHERE ctk.contract_id = bc.contract_id\n                     AND ctk.term_kind IN ('auto_renewal', 'rolling_term')\n                 )\n               )\n        )\n        SELECT counterparty,\n               contract_id,\n               COALESCE(open_invoice_ids, '') AS open_invoice_ids,\n               obligation_kinds,\n               CASE\n                 WHEN EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                   WHERE ij.disposition = 'UNRESOLVED'\n                     AND (ij.left_id = counterparty OR ij.right_id = counterparty)\n                 ) THEN 'unresolved'\n                 ELSE 'dependent'\n               END AS status\n        FROM base\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"contract_clause\",\n            \"contract_term_kind\",\n            \"contract_active\",\n            \"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\",\n            \"identity_judgment\",\n        ],\n    )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE, universe=\"contract_clause\"\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"invoice_contract_link\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE, universe=\"invoice_record\"\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"purpose_b_link\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE, universe=\"identity_judgment\"\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"purpose_c_dependency\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n )\n\n\ndef load_contracts(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    for contract_id, spec in CONTRACT_SPECS.items():\n        ref = f\"contract:{contract_id}\"\n        path =          {\"contract\": ref, \"term_kind\": term_kind},\n                grounding=[Grounding(GroundingKind.SOURCE, path, \"term\")],\n            )\n        for clause_kind in spec[\"clauses\"]:\n            tv.assert_tuple(\n                \"contract_clause\",\n                {\"contract\": ref, \"clause_kind\": clause_kind},\n                grounding=[Grounding(GroundingKind.SOURCE, path, Grounding(\n                    GroundingKind.SOURCE,\n                    path,\n                    f\"active as of {EVAL_DATE}\",\n                )\n            ],\n        )\n\n\ndef load_candidates(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    registry_norms: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for row in tv.query('SELECT company_id, legal_name FROM registry_record'):\n        registry_norms[row[\"company_id\"]] = normalize_name(row[\"legal_name\"])\n\n    contract_counterparty_norm: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for row in tv.query(\n        \"SELECT contract_id, counterparty_text FROM contract_record\"\n    ):\n        contract_counterparty_norm[row[\"contract_id\"]] = normalize_name(\n            row[\"counterparty_text\"]\n        )\n\n    for cluster in COMMERCIAL_CLUSTERS:\n        crm_ref = f\"crm:{cluster['crm']}\"\n        billing_ref = f\"billing:{cluster['billing_name']}\"\n        contract_ref = f\"contract:{cluster['contract']}\"\n        tv.assert_tuple(\n            \"crm_to_billing_candidate\",\n            {\"crm_account\": crm_ref, \"billing_name\": billing_ref},\n            grounding=[\n                Grounding(\n                    GroundingKind.SOURCE,\n                    \"commercial_notes.md\",\n                    f\"cluster {cluster['crm']}\",\n                )\n            ],\n        )\n        billing_norm = normalize_name(cluster[\"billing_name\"])\n        for company_number in cluster[\"registry\"]:\n            registry_ref = f\"registry:{company_number}\"\n            reg_norm = registry_norms[registry_ref]\n            if billing_norm == reg_norm or billing_norm in reg_norm or reg_norm in billing_norm:\n                tv.assert_tuple(\n                    \"billing_to_registry_candidate\",\n                    {\n                        \"billing_name\": billing_ref,\n                        \"registry_company\": registry_ref,\n                    },\n                    grounding=[\n                        Grounding(\n                            GroundingKind.SOURCE,\n                            \"company_registry.csv\",\n                            company_number,\n                        )\n                    ],\n                )\n        cp_norm = contract_counterparty_norm[contract_ref]\n        crm_rows = tv.query(\n            \"SELECT         (billing_ref, billing_norm),\n            (crm_ref, crm_norm),\n        ):\n            if norm == cp_norm or norm in cp_norm or cp_norm in norm:\n                tv.assert_tuple(\n                    \"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\",\n                    {\"counterparty\": counterparty_ref, \"contract\": contract_ref},\n                    grounding=[\n                        Grounding(\n                            GroundingKind.SOURCE,\n                            f\"sources/contracts/{CONTRACT_SPECS[cluster['contract']]['file']}\",\n                            \"counterparty\",\n                        )\n                    ],\n                )\n\n\ndef register_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT contract_id, clause_kind\n        FROM contract_clause\n        WHERE clause_kind IN (\n            'change_of_control_consent',\n            'change_of_control_termination',\n            'assignment_consent_required',\n            'assignment_notice_required',\n            'assignment_competitor_prohibition'\n        )\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"contract_clause\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"invoice_contract_link\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        WITH invoice_billing AS (\n            SELECT invoice_id, 'billing:' || billed_name AS billing_ref\n            FROM invoice_record\n        ),\n        billing_contract AS (\n            SELECT ctc.counterparty_id AS billing_ref, ctc.contract_id\n            FROM counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n            WHERE ctc.counterparty_id LIKE 'billing:%'\n              AND NOT EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                WHERE ij.disposition = 'DISTINCT'\n                  AND (\n                    (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                    OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                  )\n              )\n              AND (\n                EXISTS (\n                  SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                  WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n                    AND (\n                      (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                      OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                    )\n                )\n                OR NOT EXISTS (\n                  SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                  WHERE (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                     OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                )\n              )\n        )\n        SELECT ib.invoice_id,\n               bc.contract_id,\n               CASE\n                 WHEN EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                   WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n                     AND (\n                       (ij.left_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.right_id = bc.contract_id)\n                       OR (ij.right_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.left_id = bc.contract_id)\n                     )\n                 ) THEN 'asserted'\n                 WHEN EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                   WHERE ij.disposition = 'UNRESOLVED'\n                     AND (\n                       (ij.left_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.right_id = bc.contract_id)\n                       OR (ij.right_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.left_id = bc.contract_id)\n                     )\n                 ) THEN 'unresolved'\n                 ELSE 'asserted'\n               END AS association\n        FROM invoice_billing ib\n        JOIN billing_contract bc ON bc.billing_ref = ib.billing_ref\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"identity_judgment\",\n            \"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_a_invoice\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT ir.invoice_id,\n               ir.billed_name,\n               ir.amount,\n               ir.currency,\n               ir.period,\n               ir.status,\n               icl.contract_id,\n               icl.association\n        FROM invoice_record ir\n        JOIN invoice_contract_link icl ON icl.invoice_id = ir.invoice_id\n        JOIN contract_active ca ON ca.contract_id = icl.contract_id AND ca.active = 1\n        WHERE EXISTS (\n            SELECT 1 FROM acquisition_relevant_clause arc\n            WHERE arc.contract_id = icl.contract_id\n        )\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"invoice_contract_link\",\n            \"contract_active\",\n            \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_b_link\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT ij.left_id AS left,\n               ij.right_id AS right,\n               ij.disposition AS epistemic\n        FROM identity_judgment ij\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"identity_judgment\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_c_dependency\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        WITH open_invoices AS (\n            SELECT 'billing:' || billed_name AS counterparty,\n                   replace(invoice_id, 'invoice:', '') AS invoice_short\n            FROM invoice_record\n            WHERE status = 'open'\n        ),\n        obligations AS (\n            SELECT contract_id, clause_kind AS kind\n            FROM contract_clause\n            WHERE clause_kind = 'exclusivity'\n            UNION\n            SELECT contract_id, term_kind AS kind\n            FROM contract_term_kind\n            WHERE term_kind IN ('auto_renewal', 'rolling_term')\n        ),\n        billing_contract AS (\n            SELECT ctc.counterparty_id AS counterparty, ctc.contract_id\n            FROM counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n            WHERE ctc.counterparty_id LIKE 'billing:%'\n              AND EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n                  AND (\n                    (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                    OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                  )\n              )\n        ),\n        base AS (\n            SELECT bc.counterparty,\n                   bc.contract_id,\n                   GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT oi.invoice_short) AS open_invoice_ids,\n                   GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT ob.kind) AS obligation_kinds\n            FROM billing_contract bc\n            JOIN contract_active ca ON ca.contract_id = bc.contract_id AND ca.active = 1\n            JOIN obligations ob ON ob.contract_id = bc.contract_id\n            LEFT JOIN open_invoices oi ON oi.counterparty = bc.counterparty\n            GROUP BY bc.counterparty, bc.contract_id\n            HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT ob.kind) >= 1\n               AND (\n                 COUNT(DISTINCT oi.invoice_short) > 0\n                 OR EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM contract_term_kind ctk\n                   WHERE ctk.contract_id = bc.contract_id\n                     AND ctk.term_kind IN ('auto_renewal', 'rolling_term')\n                 )\n               )\n        )\n        SELECT counterparty,\n               contract_id,\n               COALESCE(open_invoice_ids, '') AS open_invoice_ids,\n               obligation_kinds,\n               CASE\n                 WHEN EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                   WHERE ij.disposition = 'UNRESOLVED'\n                     AND (ij.left_id = counterparty OR ij.right_id = counterparty)\n                 ) THEN 'unresolved'\n                 ELSE 'dependent'\n               END AS status\n        FROM base\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"contract_clause\",\n            \"contract_term_kind\",\n            \"contract_active\",\n            \"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\",\n            \"identity_judgment\",\n        ],\n    )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE, universe=\"contract_clause\"\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"invoice_contract_link\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE, universe=\"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\"\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"purpose_a_invoice\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE, universe=\"invoice_record\"\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"purpose_b_link\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE, universe=\"identity_judgment\"\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"purpose_c_dependency\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE, universe=\"contract_active\"\n        ),\n    )\n\n\ndef compile_mechanical(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    declare_world_schema(tv)\n    declare_derived_schema(tv)\n \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"contract_clause\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"invoice_contract_link\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        WITH invoice_billing AS (\n            SELECT \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT contract_id, clause_kind\n        FROM contract_clause\n        WHERE clause_kind IN (\n            'change_of_control_consent',\n            'change_of_control_termination',\n            'assignment_consent_required',\n            'assignment_notice_required',\n            'assignment_competitor_prohibition'\n        )\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"contract_clause\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"invoice_contract_link\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        WITH invoice_billing AS (\n            SELECT invoice_id, 'billing:' || billed_name AS billing_ref\n            FROM invoice_record\n        ),\n        billing_contract AS (\n            SELECT ij.left_id AS billing_ref, ij.right_id AS contract_id\n            FROM identity_judgment ij\n            WHERE ij.left_id LIKE 'billing:%'\n              AND ij.right_id LIKE 'contract:%'\n              AND ij.disposition IN ('SAME_ENTITY', 'UNRESOLVED')\n            UNION\n            SELECT ij.right_id AS billing_ref, ij.left_id AS contract_id\n            FROM identity_judgment ij\n            WHERE ij.right_id LIKE 'billing:%'\n              AND ij.left_id LIKE 'contract:%'\n              AND ij.disposition IN ('SAME_ENTITY', 'UNRESOLVED')\n            UNION\n            SELECT ctc.counterparty_id AS billing_ref, ctc.contract_id\n            FROM counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n            WHERE ctc.counterparty_id LIKE 'billing:%'\n              AND NOT EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                WHERE (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                   OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n              )\n        )\n        SELECT ib.invoice_id,\n               bc.contract_id,\n               CASE\n                 WHEN EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                   WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n                     AND (\n                       (ij.left_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.right_id = bc.contract_id)\n                       OR (ij.right_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.left_id = bc.contract_id)\n                     )\n                 ) THEN 'asserted'\n                 WHEN EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                   WHERE ij.disposition = 'UNRESOLVED'\n                     AND (\n                       (ij.left_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.right_id = bc.contract_id)\n                       OR (ij.right_id = ib.billing_ref AND ij.left_id = bc.contract_id)\n                     )\n                 ) THEN 'unresolved'\n                 ELSE 'asserted'\n               END AS association\n        FROM invoice_billing ib\n        JOIN billing_contract bc ON bc.billing_ref = ib.billing_ref\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"identity_judgment\",\n            \"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_a_invoice\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT ir.invoice_id,\n               ir.billed_name,\n               ir.amount,\n               ir.currency,\n               ir.period,\n               ir.status,\n               icl.contract_id,\n               icl.association\n        FROM invoice_record ir\n        JOIN invoice_contract_link icl ON icl.invoice_id = ir.invoice_id\n        JOIN contract_active ca ON ca.contract_id = icl.contract_id AND ca.active = 1\n        WHERE EXISTS (\n            SELECT 1 FROM acquisition_relevant_clause arc\n            WHERE arc.contract_id = icl.contract_id\n        )\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"invoice_contract_link\",\n            \"contract_active\",\n            \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        ],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_b_link\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        SELECT ij.left_id AS left,\n               ij.right_id AS right,\n               ij.disposition AS epistemic\n        FROM identity_judgment ij\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\"identity_judgment\"],\n    )\n    tv.register_derivation(\n        \"purpose_c_dependency\",\n        sql=\"\"\"\n        WITH open_invoices AS (\n            SELECT 'billing:' || billed_name AS counterparty,\n                   replace(invoice_id, 'invoice:', '') AS invoice_short\n            FROM invoice_record\n            WHERE status = 'open'\n        ),\n        obligations AS (\n            SELECT contract_id, clause_kind AS kind\n            FROM contract_clause\n            WHERE clause_kind = 'exclusivity'\n            UNION\n            SELECT contract_id, term_kind AS kind\n            FROM contract_term_kind\n            WHERE term_kind IN ('auto_renewal', 'rolling_term')\n        ),\n        billing_contract AS (\n            SELECT ctc.counterparty_id AS counterparty, ctc.contract_id\n            FROM counterparty_to_contract_candidate ctc\n            WHERE ctc.counterparty_id LIKE 'billing:%'\n              AND EXISTS (\n                SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                WHERE ij.disposition = 'SAME_ENTITY'\n                  AND (\n                    (ij.left_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.right_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                    OR (ij.right_id = ctc.counterparty_id AND ij.left_id = ctc.contract_id)\n                  )\n              )\n        ),\n        base AS (\n            SELECT bc.counterparty,\n                   bc.contract_id,\n                   GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT oi.invoice_short) AS open_invoice_ids,\n                   GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT ob.kind) AS obligation_kinds\n            FROM billing_contract bc\n            JOIN contract_active ca ON ca.contract_id = bc.contract_id AND ca.active = 1\n            JOIN obligations ob ON ob.contract_id = bc.contract_id\n            LEFT JOIN open_invoices oi ON oi.counterparty = bc.counterparty\n            GROUP BY bc.counterparty, bc.contract_id\n            HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT ob.kind) >= 1\n               AND (\n                 COUNT(DISTINCT oi.invoice_short) > 0\n                 OR EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM contract_term_kind ctk\n                   WHERE ctk.contract_id = bc.contract_id\n                     AND ctk.term_kind IN ('auto_renewal', 'rolling_term')\n                 )\n               )\n        )\n        SELECT counterparty,\n               contract_id,\n               COALESCE(open_invoice_ids, '') AS open_invoice_ids,\n               obligation_kinds,\n               CASE\n                 WHEN EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM identity_judgment ij\n                   WHERE ij.disposition = 'UNRESOLVED'\n                     AND (ij.left_id = counterparty OR ij.right_id = counterparty)\n                 ) THEN 'unresolved'\n                 ELSE 'dependent'\n               END AS status\n        FROM base\n        \"\"\",\n        inputs=[\n            \"invoice_record\",\n            \"contract_clause\",\n            \"contract_term_kind\",\n            \"contract_active\",\n            \"counterparty_to_contract_candidate\",\n            \"identity_judgment\",\n        ],\n    )\n\n\ndef run_derivations(tv: TaskView) -> None:\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"acquisition_relevant_clause\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE, universe=\"contract_clause\"\n        ),\n    )\n    tv.run_derivation(\n        \"invoice_contract_link\",\n        completeness=Completeness(\n            CompletenessStatus.COMPLETE,
